\vspace{16pt}
\begin{tablenotes}[flushleft]
\footnotesize\setlength{\itemsep}{3pt}\setlength{\labelsep}{0pt}
\item[] Turnout is \% of the group's registered voters.
\item[] BN, PH and PN are \% of the group's valid votes statewide (inc. zeroes in seats it did not contest).
\item[] $\pm$ is the out-of-sample validation error, measured against near-pure salurans ($\geq$95\% one group); it is not a confidence interval.
\item[] Small superscript and subscript figures are the upper and lower ends of a partial-identification range. This method of quantifying uncertainty is used where no out-of-sample test is possible at 90\% concentration. The point estimate is not necessarily the band's midpoint.
\end{tablenotes}
